Betray thy earthly kin, share not thy voice." ending "Grant me the blessing of lightning—Zalga Yell!!!", used as an opening salvo.[@school-district-spells.so13-yell] The Japanese chant is {{ja|雷よ、天の称号よ。地の血統に背信すべく、汝の声を分け与えん。駆ける我が身に雷名の祝福を|ikazuchi yo, ten no shōgō yo. chi no kettō ni haishin subeku, nanji no koe o wakeataen. kakeru waga mi ni raimei no shukufuku o}}, *O lightning, O title of heaven: that I may betray my earthly blood, share out thy voice; upon my running body, the blessing of the thunder-name*: the second line asks the lightning to share its voice, not to withhold it.[@school-district-spells.so13-yell]

#### Silva Vine (Miliria) {#school-district-spells-silva-vine-miliria}

Miliria, a forest hunter of the 7th Squad whose best abilities are Agility and Dexterity, casts it with the basic Concurrent Casting Lefiya taught her. Her chant opens "Sprout new leaves and verdant light." As she runs she touches walls and rocks, "like planting a seed", leaving small magic circles; when she activates the spell, whips of light sprout from all of them at once and bind the pursuing monsters.[@school-district-spells.so13-vine]

#### Open questions {#school-district-spells-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - The students' full chants and Status entries.[@school-district-spells.fm19-mine, school-district-spells.so13-amalda]

#### Printed chant words {#school-district-spells-printed-chants}

**Zalga Amalda — printed chant, excluding the instruction to Luke (SO13).**[@school-district-spells.so13-amalda]

> Growling torrent gleam, blessed censure. Shatter the noble with thy splendor. Swallow, jaws of lightning.

**Zalga Yell — printed chant and name (SO13).**[@school-district-spells.so13-yell]

> Lightning, heaven’s name. Betray thy earthly kin, share not thy voice. Grant me the blessing of lightning—Zalga Yell!!!

**Silva Vine — both printed chant lines (SO13).**[@school-district-spells.so13-vine]

> Sprout new leaves and verdant light. Grow, grow, grow, ascend trees, dampen flowers, adorn the forest. And bind. Admonish the savages. This is the forest shrine kept by its guardian.

### Innate elven magic: Gale Blast and Flare Burn {#elven-innate-magic}

Elves have an innate magic, "formalized through repeated studies and rituals stretching back to ancient times", cast through long-form spells and usable without a god's blessing.[@elven-innate-magic.so14-chase] Sword Oratoria 14 shows two such spells when the knights of the royal forest chase the young [[riveria|Riveria]], who has fled on a unicorn.[@elven-innate-magic.so14-chase] See also [[#innate-and-acquired-magic|Innate and acquired magic]].

- **Casters:** Elven knights of the royal forest; the young [[riveria|Riveria]][@elven-innate-magic.so14-chase]
- **Type:** Innate magic, usable without a blessing[@elven-innate-magic.so14-chase]
- **Chants:** More than a dozen stanzas[@elven-innate-magic.so14-chase]

#### Incantation {#elven-innate-magic-incantation}

**Gale Blast — ending only; the earlier stanzas are not printed (SO14).**[@elven-innate-magic.so14-chase]

> By ancient compact I summon you, winds of nature. Heed my call and mow down my enemies!

**Flare Burn — ending only; the earlier stanzas are not printed (SO14).**[@elven-innate-magic.so14-chase]

> Gh…By ancient compact I summon you, inferno of earth. Heed my call and scorch all violence!

Two chant endings are printed before the names, one calling on "winds of nature" and one on the "inferno of earth"; each begins "By ancient compact I summon you".[@elven-innate-magic.so14-chase] The narration says the spells need "more than a dozen stanzas".[@elven-innate-magic.so14-chase] In the Japanese the endings are {{ja|契約に応えよ、森羅の風よ。我が命に従い敵対者を薙げ|keiyaku ni kotaeyo, shinra no kaze yo. waga mei ni shitagai tekitaisha o nage}}, *Answer the compact, O wind of all creation; obey my command and mow down the foe*, and {{ja|契約に応えよ、大地の焰よ。我が命に従い暴力を焼き払え|keiyaku ni kotaeyo, daichi no homura yo. waga mei ni shitagai bōryoku o yakiharae}}, *Answer the compact, O flame of the earth; obey my command and burn away violence*.[@elven-innate-magic.so14-chase]

#### The spells {#elven-innate-magic-the-spells}

- **Gale Blast** and **Flare Burn** are called out one after the other. The knights chose wind magic, taking care not to injure the princess, while Riveria chose fire.[@elven-innate-magic.so14-chase] {{inference}} By their names, Gale Blast is the knights' wind spell and Flare Burn Riveria's fire spell.
- **Equestrian casting:** the knights cast from horseback, letting their steeds pursue and evade while they chant, a pseudo-Concurrent Casting developed although they are not master mages. Riveria had taught herself the same technique on her hunts, and though she started later, her faster incantation finished first.[@elven-innate-magic.so14-chase]
- **Outcome:** Riveria uses the burst of heat from her flames to dodge the gusts, but the wind knocks down her attendant Aina's horse, and an arrow brings down Riveria's unicorn.[@elven-innate-magic.so14-chase]

#### Open questions {#elven-innate-magic-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - Which printed chant ending belongs to which caster.[@elven-innate-magic.so14-chase]

## Spells of Freya Familia {#freya-familia-spells}

### Hildis Vini {#hildis-vini}

**Hildis Vini** ({{ja|黄金猪の毛皮}}, written *the golden boar's hide*)[@hildis-vini.fm18-ja] is [[ottar|Ottar]]'s one and only magic.[@hildis-vini.fc02-udaeus] It is a simple enhancement spell: golden light covers him and his weapon and raises the weapon's power, and with Ottar's strength behind it the result is devastating.[@hildis-vini.ar03-zald, hildis-vini.fm18-slash]

- **User:** [[ottar|Ottar]][@hildis-vini.fc02-udaeus]
- **Type:** Enhancement (not even an enchantment)[@hildis-vini.fm18-slash]
- **Status entry:** "???"; no description printed[@hildis-vini.fc02-sheet]
- **Effect:** Raises his weapon's power[@hildis-vini.ar03-zald, hildis-vini.fm20-afterglow]
- **Chant:** Short; three sentences[@hildis-vini.fc02-udaeus, hildis-vini.fm18-bell]

#### Incantation {#hildis-vini-incantation}

**Complete printed chant (FC02).**[@hildis-vini.fc02-udaeus]

> Silver moon’s mercy and the golden plains. I offer this body to the lord of battle. Charge bearing the goddess’s will.

{{nocite}} **Notes and other printed variants**

{{nocite}} The chant is short, three sentences, and is printed with the same words in every volume that shows it. 

- **Opening (excerpt):** "Silver moon’s mercy and the golden plains."[@hildis-vini.fc02-udaeus, hildis-vini.fm18-bell]
- **Middle:** he offers his body to the lord of battle.[@hildis-vini.fc02-udaeus]
- **Last line:** "Charge bearing the goddess’s will."[@hildis-vini.fc02-udaeus, hildis-vini.fm18-bell]
- **Release:** "Hildis Vini."[@hildis-vini.fc02-udaeus, hildis-vini.fm18-bell]

Familia Chronicle 2 and DanMachi 18 both call it a short cast.[@hildis-vini.fc02-udaeus, hildis-vini.fm18-bell] Astrea Record 3 prints the same words with exclamation marks.[@hildis-vini.ar03-zald] Later in DanMachi 18 the chant is printed piece by piece while Ottar fights.[@hildis-vini.fm18-casting]

#### Effect {#hildis-vini-effect}

Ottar describes it himself in Astrea Record 3: golden light envelops his body and his sword, turning it into "a blade of radiant light"; the spell "did nothing but raise my weapon's power", but his strength crossed with his magic makes a force no one can stand against.[@hildis-vini.ar03-zald]

DanMachi 18 insists that it is "a simple enhancement spell" that "wasn’t even an enchantment", so it should not let him hit anything beyond his blade's reach. Yet his enhanced slash cuts through the air and blows away everything in its path.[@hildis-vini.fm18-slash] In DanMachi 20 [[leon-verdenberg|Leon]] explains the reason: his and Ottar's enhancement magic only increases physical strength and weapon power, and it is a separate technique, the *afterglow*, that lets them "erase the distance" and strike far-off targets.[@hildis-vini.fm20-afterglow]

#### Casting it in battle {#hildis-vini-casting-it-in-battle}

In DanMachi 18 Ottar chants while fighting three opponents. He is not an elf like Hedin or Hegni and cannot attack, move, dodge and chant all at once as Lyu does; like Mikoto, he adds only one other action, and he chooses defence, planting his foot and blocking everything while he casts.[@hildis-vini.fm18-casting] The narration adds that he seldom uses the spell because it is "so terribly fuel-inefficient", and his raw strength is usually enough.[@hildis-vini.fm18-casting]

#### Notable uses {#hildis-vini-notable-uses}

| Volume | Use |
|---|---|
| Familia Chronicle 2 | Against the floor boss Udaeus: Ottar catches and crushes a pilum thrown to interrupt his chant, finishes it, and delivers the blow that destroys Udaeus; his greatsword shatters.[@hildis-vini.fc02-udaeus, hildis-vini.fc02-after] |
| Astrea Record 3 | His final clash with [[zald|Zald]] in the Great Conflict: the collision of gold and fire shakes the city and brings the ice wall and barrier down, and Ottar wins.[@hildis-vini.ar03-zald, hildis-vini.ar03-clash, hildis-vini.ar03-win] |
| DanMachi 18 | Meets Bell's five-minute Argo Vesta; both swords shatter. Ottar calls it a draw, then says that, considering their difference in Level, Bell's attack surpassed his.[@hildis-vini.fm18-bell, hildis-vini.fm18-draw] |
| DanMachi 18 | Its golden slash drives Lyu, Bell and Mia apart in the amphitheatre.[@hildis-vini.fm18-slash] |

#### Open questions {#hildis-vini-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - Its Mind cost, how long it lasts, and whether it always breaks the weapon it is used with.[@hildis-vini.fm18-casting, hildis-vini.fc02-after]

### Glarinese Fromel {#glarinese-fromel}

**Glarinese Fromel** is [[allen-fromel|Allen Fromel]]'s one and only magic.[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-effect] It gives him superspeed that keeps increasing the longer he runs, and his strength rises with his speed, so that the faster he goes the more destructive his charge becomes.[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-effect] He had never recited it in front of his sister, and [[anya-fromel|Anya]] learns in the DanMachi 18 Familia War that he has magic at all.[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-chant]

- **User:** [[allen-fromel|Allen Fromel]], his only magic[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-effect]
- **Effect:** Ever-increasing speed; power rises with speed[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-effect]
- **Chant:** Several lines, printed in full[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-chant]

#### Incantation {#glarinese-fromel-incantation}

**Complete printed chant (FM18).**[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-chant]

> Golden wheel, silver collar. Hated love, illusory corpse, thy destiny is here. Be gone, gold wheel, before the rut kills you. Whip of honor, lips of favor, thy payment is here. Spin, silver wheel, until thy head falls. Run bearing the goddess’s will—until death and distant heavens when you can hear the wheel’s song again.

{{nocite}} **Notes and other printed variants**

DanMachi 18 prints the chant from its first words, where Allen "began singing the chariot’s song", to its last lines and the name, with narration between the pieces.[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-chant] 

- **Opening:** "Golden wheel, silver collar."[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-chant]
- **Second line (excerpt):** "Hated love, illusory corpse, thy destiny is here."[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-chant]
- **Third line (excerpt):** "Whip of honor, lips of favor, thy payment is here."[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-chant]
- **Last lines (excerpt):** "Run bearing the goddess’s will—until death and distant heavens"[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-chant]
- **Name:** "Glarinese Fromel!"[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-chant]

The narration calls it a "disgraceful spell that reflected the depths of his heart" and what he truly felt toward his sister.[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-chant]

#### Effect {#glarinese-fromel-effect}

Allen is swathed in a silver-blue gleam and runs like a chariot across the battlefield, accelerating the more he runs.[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-chant] The magic massively raises his agility and gives him strength in proportion to his speed; the narration states there is no upper limit, so in theory the force of his charge grows as far as he can accelerate. Clad in "an armor of light", he could run down even a monster rex.[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-effect]

- **Casting while moving:** he chants while leaping backward again and again, putting tens of meders between himself and his attackers until the song is finished.[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-chant]
- **Control:** even Allen cannot control the speed perfectly; his swerving track sweeps up adventurers on both sides.[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-chant]

#### Limits {#glarinese-fromel-limits}

Glarinese Fromel has to be chanted, and Allen has to slow down to do it. On his second attempt in DanMachi 18 [[welf-crozzo|Welf]]'s [[#will-o-the-wisp|Will-o'-the-Wisp]] turns the gathering magic into an Ignis Fatuus before the spell completes.[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-second]

#### Notable uses {#glarinese-fromel-notable-uses}

| Volume | Use |
|---|---|
| DanMachi 18 | Against Anya and [[hegni|Hegni]]: the charge throws Hegni into the air, wounds Anya and runs through Aisha, Mikoto, [[miach-familia#nahza-ersuisu|Nahza]], Runoa, Chloe and even Alfrik Gulliver.[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-chant] |
| DanMachi 18 | Racing Bell toward the house of the gods, Allen starts the chant again, but Welf's anti-magic fire sets off an Ignis Fatuus in him and his guards.[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-second] |

#### Open questions {#glarinese-fromel-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - How long the effect lasts once cast, and its Mind cost.[@glarinese-fromel.fm18-effect]

### Dáinsleif {#dainsleif}

**Dáinsleif** ({{ja|黒妖の魔剣}}, written *the dark fairy's magic sword*)[@dainsleif.fm16-ja] is [[hegni|Hegni Ragnar]]'s magic. It changes his personality, letting the anxious, weak-willed elf "embody the mental image he had of himself" and become a merciless warrior king.[@dainsleif.fc02-cast] His title, also Dáinsleif, comes from the name of this magic.[@dainsleif.fc02-cast, dainsleif.fm18-cast]

{{nocite}} Not to be confused with a sword of the same name:

- In Minor Myths and Legends 1 a back-alley dealer shows [[aiz-wallenstein|Aiz]] a sword called Dáinsleif; the text does not connect it with Hegni.[@dainsleif.ss01-sword]

- **User:** [[hegni|Hegni Ragnar]][@dainsleif.fc02-cast]
- **Type:** Rare magic that alters the caster's personality[@dainsleif.fc02-cast, dainsleif.fm18-cast]
- **Status effect:** None[@dainsleif.fc02-cast, dainsleif.fm18-cast]
- **Chant:** Short; three sentences[@dainsleif.fc02-cast]

#### Incantation {#dainsleif-incantation}

**Complete printed chant (FC02).**[@dainsleif.fc02-cast]

> Draw thine sword, King of the fiendish blades. Sacrifice reason and offer up blood. Slaughter all until the feast is finished.

{{nocite}} **Notes and other printed variants**

Familia Chronicle 2 prints the whole chant, three sentences, and calls it a short cast.[@dainsleif.fc02-cast] 

- **Opening:** "Draw thine sword, King of the fiendish blades."[@dainsleif.fc02-cast]
- **Then:** "Sacrifice reason and offer up blood. Slaughter all until the feast is finished."[@dainsleif.fc02-cast, dainsleif.fm18-cast]
- **Name:** "Dáinsleif."[@dainsleif.fc02-cast, dainsleif.fm18-cast]

DanMachi 18 prints the last two sentences and the name; Astrea Record 3 prints only the last sentence with the name.[@dainsleif.fm18-cast, dainsleif.ar03-cast] In DanMachi 16, when he attacks [[bell-cranell|Bell]], the last sentence is printed before the name: "Slaughter all until the feast is finished."[@dainsleif.fm16-bell]

Hegni plants his black sword in the ground and a black magic circle spreads around it; when he speaks the name, the circle shatters and its light is absorbed into his body.[@dainsleif.fc02-cast]

#### Effect {#dainsleif-effect}

- **Personality and speech:** the change "surpassed autosuggestion" and is a genuine remaking of himself: his personality and vocabulary become those of another person.[@dainsleif.fc02-cast, dainsleif.fm18-cast]
- **No stronger Status:** it resembles "a certain prum hero’s fighting spirit buff magic" but does not increase his Status; it "merely manipulated his personality".[@dainsleif.fc02-cast] {{inference}} The prum hero is [[finn-deimne|Finn]], and the spell [[#hell-finegas|Hell Finegas]]. DanMachi 18 agrees that it "didn't affect his status in any way", yet both volumes say it removes the limiter that holds back his full strength.[@dainsleif.fc02-cast, dainsleif.fm18-cast]
- **The same man underneath:** Minor Myths and Legends 2 says Hegni is still the same person at his core; "Dáinsleif" is a convenient name for his alter ego, and at times the two sets of thoughts get mixed.[@dainsleif.ss02-core]

#### Limits {#dainsleif-limits}

- **Kept active:** to maintain his other self he spends Mind constantly, and the magic power shows as a purple haze and a light in his eyes. Because it must stay active like an enchantment, [[welf-crozzo|Welf]] sees it as a target for [[#will-o-the-wisp|Will-o'-the-Wisp]], but [[allen-fromel|Allen]] cuts him down before the fire takes effect.[@dainsleif.fm18-welf]
- **It wears off:** late in the Great Conflict of Astrea Record 3 it "had finally worn off".[@dainsleif.ar03-worn]
- **By choice:** in DanMachi 18 Hegni deliberately declares his change of side without activating it, as a show of resolve.[@dainsleif.fm18-resolve]

#### Notable uses {#dainsleif-notable-uses}

| Volume | Use |
|---|---|
| DanMachi 16 | During the festival he attacks Bell in a deserted alley.[@dainsleif.fm16-bell] |
| Familia Chronicle 2 | In the desert war he casts it before ten thousand enemies and cuts through the Warsa soldiers.[@dainsleif.fc02-cast] |
| Astrea Record 3 | Against [[apate-and-alecto-familias#apate-familia|Basram]]'s spirit warriors in the Great Conflict.[@dainsleif.ar03-cast] |
| DanMachi 18 | Casting it despite a hail of magic swords and arrows, he cuts through [[tsubaki|Tsubaki]]'s adventurers and smiths; Tsubaki knows he once felled over a thousand of the Evils in one battle with it.[@dainsleif.fm18-cast] [[lyu-leon|Lyu]] later defeats him, and Orario roars at his defeat.[@dainsleif.fm18-lyu] |

#### Open questions {#dainsleif-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - How long it lasts and how much Mind it takes to maintain.[@dainsleif.fm18-welf, dainsleif.ar03-worn]

### Burn Dáin {#burn-dain}

**Burn Dáin** is a fire spell of [[hegni|Hegni Ragnar]]'s: "a short-range explosive fire spell" whose range is traded for enough destructive force to incinerate every enemy inside its area.[@burn-dain.fc02-ottar, burn-dain.fm18-allen]

- **User:** [[hegni|Hegni Ragnar]][@burn-dain.fc02-ottar]
- **Type:** Short-range explosive fire[@burn-dain.fc02-ottar, burn-dain.fm18-allen]
- **Chant:** One sentence (super-short)[@burn-dain.fc02-ottar, burn-dain.fm18-allen]

#### Incantation {#burn-dain-incantation}

Both prints give the same single sentence, then the name, and both call it a super-short cast.[@burn-dain.fc02-ottar, burn-dain.fm18-allen]

- **Chant:** "By the power of the demon blade, bring eternal destruction."[@burn-dain.fc02-ottar, burn-dain.fm18-allen]
- **Name:** "Burn Dáin."[@burn-dain.fc02-ottar] DanMachi 18 prints it with an exclamation mark.[@burn-dain.fm18-allen]

#### Effect {#burn-dain-effect}

An eruption of flame pours from his outstretched arm, lit brighter by the black magic circle at his feet.[@burn-dain.fc02-ottar] In DanMachi 18 it strikes [[allen-fromel|Allen]] "at super-close range" and blows him away; he kicks off the ground to evade, but, unwounded until then, he is hit.[@burn-dain.fm18-allen]

#### Notable uses {#burn-dain-notable-uses}

| Volume | Use |
|---|---|
| Familia Chronicle 2 | Cut in from the side against [[ottar|Ottar]], who meets the blaze with a full upward swing of his greatsword.[@burn-dain.fc02-ottar] |
| DanMachi 18 | Hegni whispers the chant behind the collar of his cloak, so Allen cannot see his lips move, and catches him as his spear is about to land.[@burn-dain.fm18-allen] |

#### Open questions {#burn-dain-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - Its exact range and Mind cost.[@burn-dain.fm18-allen]

### Caurus Hildr {#caurus-hildr}

**Caurus Hildr** is a lightning spell of [[hedin|Hedin Selrand]]'s: a single stanza, "a super-short cast specialized for quick attacks", that brings down a rain of white lightning.[@caurus-hildr.fc02-warsa] It is the barrage among his three lightning spells; [[#valiant-hildr|Valiant Hildr]] gathers the same power into one blast.[@caurus-hildr.ar01-street]

- **User:** [[hedin|Hedin Selrand]][@caurus-hildr.fc02-warsa]
- **Type:** Lightning barrage; super-short cast[@caurus-hildr.fc02-warsa]
- **Chant:** One sentence[@caurus-hildr.fc02-warsa, caurus-hildr.ar01-street]

#### Incantation {#caurus-hildr-incantation}

The chant is one sentence, and every volume that prints it in full uses the same words, sometimes with an exclamation mark.[@caurus-hildr.fc02-warsa, caurus-hildr.ar01-street, caurus-hildr.fm18-heith]

- **Chant:** "Struggle for eternity, indestructible soldiers of lightning."[@caurus-hildr.fc02-warsa, caurus-hildr.fm18-heith]
- **Name:** "Caurus Hildr."[@caurus-hildr.fc02-warsa, caurus-hildr.fm18-heith]

In the Japanese edition DanMachi 17 gives the full chant at both castings.[@caurus-hildr.fm17-training]

#### Effect {#caurus-hildr-effect}

Familia Chronicle 2 describes the lightning as orbs each the size of a human head, falling like "a thunderstorm of certain death".[@caurus-hildr.fc02-warsa] In DanMachi 17 [[bell-cranell|Bell]] sees it as bolts each the size of a person's head and calls it wide-area destruction magic.[@caurus-hildr.fm17-training]

- **Aim:** Hedin aims the bolts. In Familia Chronicle 2 he tells his targets not to move because it "messes with my aiming".[@caurus-hildr.fc02-warsa] {{statement}} In DanMachi 18 he keeps up "concentrated precision fire" from several hundred meders away, then switches to arcing the lightning up into the sky to fall on the enemy from overhead.[@caurus-hildr.fm18-gulliver]
- **Power:** In Astrea Record 3 Dina Dis thinks of it as "a low-power, precision-strike magic" that Hedin would use to avoid hitting [[hegni|Hegni]]; she is wrong about what he will cast next.[@caurus-hildr.ar03-dina] {{statement}}

#### Notable uses {#caurus-hildr-notable-uses}

| Volume | Use |
|---|---|
| Familia Chronicle 2 | In the desert war, Hedin alone bombards the Warsa forces.[@caurus-hildr.fc02-warsa] |
| Astrea Record 1 | Leaping from a belfry, he drives back Basram's spirit warriors and the Dis sisters so that Hegni and the others can be pulled out.[@caurus-hildr.ar01-street] |
| DanMachi 17 | Part of Bell's training in Folkvangr, cast one after another with Valiant Hildr.[@caurus-hildr.fm17-training] |
| DanMachi 18 | His bombardment covers [[anya-fromel|Anya]] while she casts [[#remisto-felis|Remisto Felis]].[@caurus-hildr.fm18-gulliver] Turned on the Andhrímnir, it outlasts [[heith-velvet|Heith]]'s [[#ars-gullveig|Ars Gullveig]]: the accelerating barrage overtakes her healing and she falls, and the Andhrímnir are annihilated.[@caurus-hildr.fm18-heith] The Gulliver brothers, who know how it works, use the tavern's staff and coalition fighters as shields.[@caurus-hildr.fm18-gulliver] |

#### Open questions {#caurus-hildr-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - Its range and Mind cost.[@caurus-hildr.fm18-gulliver]

### Valiant Hildr {#valiant-hildr}

**Valiant Hildr** is a lightning spell of [[hedin|Hedin Selrand]]'s. Where [[#caurus-hildr|Caurus Hildr]] is a barrage of smaller blasts, Valiant Hildr "bundled it all into one massive lightning cannon".[@valiant-hildr.ar01-street]

- **User:** [[hedin|Hedin Selrand]][@valiant-hildr.ar01-street]
- **Type:** A single, massive lightning bolt[@valiant-hildr.ar01-street, valiant-hildr.fm18-ottar]
- **Chant:** One sentence[@valiant-hildr.ar01-street, valiant-hildr.fm18-ottar]

#### Incantation {#valiant-hildr-incantation}

The chant is one sentence, printed with the same words wherever it appears, sometimes joined to the name with a dash.[@valiant-hildr.ar01-street, valiant-hildr.fm18-ottar, valiant-hildr.fc02-ottar]

- **Chant:** "Strike forever, indestructible lord of lightning."[@valiant-hildr.fc02-ottar, valiant-hildr.fm18-ottar]
- **Name:** "Valiant Hildr."[@valiant-hildr.fm18-ottar, valiant-hildr.ar01-street]

In DanMachi 17 Hedin casts it right after Caurus Hildr in "successive high-speed casts".[@valiant-hildr.fm17-training]

#### Effect {#valiant-hildr-effect}

The bolt is described as an enormous lightning spear, a cannon blast that fills an entire street, and a blast "big enough to consume even a floor boss".[@valiant-hildr.fm17-training, valiant-hildr.ar01-street, valiant-hildr.fm18-ottar] In Familia Chronicle 2 it splits the battlefield, boils water and cuts through a crystal hill.[@valiant-hildr.fc02-ottar]

#### Notable uses {#valiant-hildr-notable-uses}

| Volume | Use |
|---|---|
| Familia Chronicle 2 | Hedin cuts short a fight between [[allen-fromel|Allen]] and [[ottar|Ottar]]; everyone, the Gullivers and Hegni included, leaps back.[@valiant-hildr.fc02-ottar] |
| Astrea Record 1 | After Caurus Hildr, it forces the Dis sisters and Basram's spirit warriors to withdraw from the crossroads.[@valiant-hildr.ar01-street] |
| Astrea Record 3 | Dina expects Caurus Hildr and gets a single great beam that engulfs her and [[hegni|Hegni]] alike.[@valiant-hildr.ar03-dina] Later Hedin kills Vena Dis with it.[@valiant-hildr.ar03-vena] |
| DanMachi 17 | Part of [[bell-cranell|Bell]]'s training in Folkvangr.[@valiant-hildr.fm17-training] |
| DanMachi 18 | A "single, concentrated blast" knocks Ottar's golden slash off course and saves Bell, Lyu and Mia; later, with Bell, Hedin fires it at Ottar himself.[@valiant-hildr.fm18-ottar] |

#### Open questions {#valiant-hildr-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - Its Mind cost and range.[@valiant-hildr.ar01-street]

### Laurus Hildr {#laurus-hildr}

**Laurus Hildr** ({{ja|聖女の雷賛}})[@laurus-hildr.fm18-ja], "the saint’s lightning eulogy", is [[hedin|Hedin Selrand]]'s third and final magic. It is a rare magic that heals the target's wounds "as if touched by a saint" and then gives them a blessing of lightning.[@laurus-hildr.fm18-bell]

- **User:** [[hedin|Hedin Selrand]], his third and final magic[@laurus-hildr.fm18-bell]
- **Type:** Rare healing enchantment[@laurus-hildr.fm18-bell]
- **Target:** Others only, whom he acknowledges[@laurus-hildr.fm18-bell]
- **Chant:** One line[@laurus-hildr.fm18-bell]

#### Incantation {#laurus-hildr-incantation}

DanMachi 18 prints one line, spoken in a failing voice, then the name.[@laurus-hildr.fm18-bell]

- **Chant:** "Sing forever…indestructible…saint."[@laurus-hildr.fm18-bell]
- **Name:** "Laurus Hildr!"[@laurus-hildr.fm18-bell]

#### Effect {#laurus-hildr-effect}

It is an enchantment: the lightning does not burn [[bell-cranell|Bell]] but envelops his whole body.[@laurus-hildr.fm18-bell] Its effect extends not only to his movement speed but to his speed of perception, and every slash of his knives carries the force of a bolt of [[#caurus-hildr|Caurus Hildr]].[@laurus-hildr.fm18-effect] In DanMachi 20 Bell compares it with Aiz's wind, calling it "the enchantment Master cast on me".[@laurus-hildr.fm20-bell]

#### Limits {#laurus-hildr-limits}

- **Not for himself:** its greatest distinguishing feature is that Hedin cannot use it on himself; he can cast it only on those he acknowledges.[@laurus-hildr.fm18-bell]
- **Cost:** in DanMachi 18 he gives it to Bell "at the cost of all his Mind".[@laurus-hildr.fm18-bell]

#### Notable uses {#laurus-hildr-notable-uses}

| Volume | Use |
|---|---|
| DanMachi 18 | Near collapse after fighting [[ottar|Ottar]], Hedin touches Bell's back and casts it on him. Together with [[haruhime|Haruhime]]'s [[#uchide-no-kozuchi|Level Boost]], it lets Bell break Ottar's stance, and its healing leaves him the only one of the group with any strength left.[@laurus-hildr.fm18-bell, laurus-hildr.fm18-effect] |

#### Open questions {#laurus-hildr-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - How long the blessing lasts, and whether the printed line is the whole chant.[@laurus-hildr.fm18-bell]

### Zeo Gullveig {#zeo-gullveig}

**Zeo Gullveig** ({{ja|ゼオ・グルヴェイグ}})[@zeo-gullveig.fm17-ja] is [[heith-velvet|Heith Velvet]]'s healing spell, "a healing spell that covered a massive area".[@zeo-gullveig.fm18-cast] With it she alone had supported the "baptism" of Freya Familia's fighters in [[folkvangr|Folkvangr]].[@zeo-gullveig.fm18-cast]

- **User:** [[heith-velvet|Heith Velvet]][@zeo-gullveig.fm18-cast]
- **Type:** Area healing[@zeo-gullveig.fm18-cast]
- **Chant:** Several lines; three printed pieces[@zeo-gullveig.fm18-cast]

#### Incantation {#zeo-gullveig-incantation}

**All three printed pieces; completeness uncertain (FM18).**[@zeo-gullveig.fm18-cast]

> —My name is Gold. Arm of the goddess who vows immortality. Thrice burned, ever pierced. Hell of flaming spears, yet light is born laying death low. Rejoice, be merry, go wild. My body is gold. Source of renewing light, bring endless conflict here.

{{nocite}} **Notes and other printed variants**

DanMachi 18 prints the chant in three pieces while the coalition watches, then the name.[@zeo-gullveig.fm18-cast] {{inference}} The first printed line opens with a dash, so it may not be the chant's beginning.

- **First printed piece (excerpt):** "—My name is Gold. Arm of the goddess who vows immortality."[@zeo-gullveig.fm18-cast]
- **Second (excerpt):** "Thrice burned, ever pierced. Hell of flaming spears, yet light is born"[@zeo-gullveig.fm18-cast]
- **Third (excerpt):** "Rejoice, be merry, go wild. My body is gold." In the Japanese she says {{ja|祝え、祝え、祝え|kurue, kurue, kurue}}, *go wild* three times; *celebrate* is the written meaning.[@zeo-gullveig.fm18-cast]
- **Name:** "Zeo Gullveig."[@zeo-gullveig.fm18-cast]

In DanMachi 17 only the name is printed.[@zeo-gullveig.fm17-bell]

#### Effect {#zeo-gullveig-effect}

A giant golden magic circle appears in the middle of the battlefield. Burns and frostbite disappear, severed limbs pressed back to wounds are restored, and fighters who had fallen get back up "like the dead rising anew".[@zeo-gullveig.fm18-cast] In Folkvangr it pulls [[bell-cranell|Bell]] back from the brink of death; Heith notes that she can heal wounds but cannot replace the blood he has lost.[@zeo-gullveig.fm17-bell]

#### Notable uses {#zeo-gullveig-notable-uses}

| Volume | Use |
|---|---|
| DanMachi 17 | In Folkvangr, Heith revives Bell during his training by Freya Familia's first-tier adventurers.[@zeo-gullveig.fm17-bell] |
| DanMachi 18 | In the Familia War it revives the einherjar that the coalition's magic swords had cut down.[@zeo-gullveig.fm18-cast] |
| Astrea Record 3 | In the Great Conflict "the Andhrímnir, Freya Familia’s formation of expert healers", unleash Zeo Gullveig over a whole arena, restoring everything in it except barricades and walls.[@zeo-gullveig.ar03-andhrimnir] The passage names the formation, not Heith. |

#### Open questions {#zeo-gullveig-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - Whether other Andhrímnir can cast Zeo Gullveig, or Astrea Record 3 simply credits the formation with Heith's spell.[@zeo-gullveig.ar03-andhrimnir]
> - Its Mind cost and the full chant.[@zeo-gullveig.fm18-cast]

### Ars Gullveig {#ars-gullveig}

**Ars Gullveig** ({{ja|アース・グルヴェイグ}}; its first word is the Japanese form of *Æsir*)[@ars-gullveig.fm18-ja] is [[heith-velvet|Heith Velvet]]'s auto-heal: "a regeneration that continuously healed damage for a set period of time".[@ars-gullveig.fm18-ars] The narration calls it a rare magic that only members of [[freya-familia|Freya Familia]] knew about.[@ars-gullveig.fm18-ars]

- **User:** [[heith-velvet|Heith Velvet]][@ars-gullveig.fm18-ars]
- **Type:** Auto-heal: regeneration for a set period[@ars-gullveig.fm18-ars]
- **Chant:** Not printed[@ars-gullveig.fm18-ars]

#### Incantation {#ars-gullveig-incantation}

The chant is not printed. In DanMachi 18 Heith had already cast it on herself and the rest of the Andhrímnir before they set foot on the battlefield: "Unfortunately, I already activated my magic."[@ars-gullveig.fm18-ars] Later she calls out the name alone while under attack.[@ars-gullveig.fm18-hedin]

#### Effect {#ars-gullveig-effect}

While it lasts, a pattern of light like a compressed magic circle appears on the skin, and golden light drives away damage as it happens.[@ars-gullveig.fm18-ars] Standing in a sea of flames, Heith and the other healers burn and regenerate at the same time until the fire can no longer hurt them; only their clothes burn away.[@ars-gullveig.fm18-ars] The coalition's adventurers compare it with the Black [[goliath|Goliath]]'s regeneration.[@ars-gullveig.fm18-ars]

#### Limits {#ars-gullveig-limits}

The narration calls it an auto-heal that "even Crozzo’s magic swords couldn’t break", and even [[hedin|Hedin]]'s [[#caurus-hildr|Caurus Hildr]] does not overcome it at once, though it is "a Level 4’s magic".[@ars-gullveig.fm18-hedin] Its weakness is Mind. Hedin tells her, "Your magic isn’t eternal. It’s finite": Heith's Mind is a cut above other healers of her Level, but Hedin, whom even Riveria acknowledges as having the highest total Mind in the city, keeps his barrage going to outlast her.[@ars-gullveig.fm18-hedin]

#### Notable uses {#ars-gullveig-notable-uses}

| Volume | Use |
|---|---|
| DanMachi 18 | Cast before the Familia War begins, it lets the Andhrímnir walk through the coalition's fire.[@ars-gullveig.fm18-ars] When Hedin turns his lightning on them, Heith declares that he cannot finish her off; he answers that her Mind will run out before his and speeds up the barrage.[@ars-gullveig.fm18-hedin] |

#### Open questions {#ars-gullveig-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - How long the "set period" is, and its Mind cost.[@ars-gullveig.fm18-ars]

### Vana Seiðr {#vana-seidr}

**Vana Seiðr** ({{ja|唯一の秘法}}, written *the sole secret art*) is [[horn|Hörn]]'s magic, a secret art only for becoming the one goddess; its effect is transformation magic, written as *god-transformation magic*.[@vana-seidr.fm16-names, vana-seidr.fm16-ja] It lets her become an exact copy of the goddess [[freya|Freya]] in every way except that she lacks [[tenkai-and-gekai#arcanum|Arcanum]].[@vana-seidr.fm16-names, vana-seidr.fm18-form] DanMachi 17 calls it "a secret spell that is hers alone".[@vana-seidr.fm17-senses]

- **User:** [[horn|Hörn]][@vana-seidr.fm16-names]
- **Type:** Transformation into a goddess[@vana-seidr.fm16-names]
- **Effect:** Becomes Freya, or Syr, without Arcanum; shared senses[@vana-seidr.fm16-names, vana-seidr.fm18-form]
- **Chant:** Several lines; three printed pieces[@vana-seidr.fm17-senses]

#### Incantation {#vana-seidr-incantation}

**All three printed pieces; original ellipses preserved (FM17).**[@vana-seidr.fm17-senses]

> …Untrodden stairs, forbidden door…today, this day, my body infringes heavens’ laws… Hollow soul, shallow lust… By the name exchanged…descend, daughter of the gods—

{{nocite}} **Notes and other printed variants**

DanMachi 17 prints the chant in three pieces as the gravely wounded Hörn casts it in [[bell-cranell|Bell]]'s arms, then the name.[@vana-seidr.fm17-senses] The narration calls it "a shorter cast" than the sage's, but one that treads on the same kind of taboo.[@vana-seidr.fm17-senses]

- **First printed piece (excerpt):** "…Untrodden stairs, forbidden door…today, this day, my body infringes heavens’ laws…"[@vana-seidr.fm17-senses]
- **Second:** "Hollow soul, shallow lust…"[@vana-seidr.fm17-senses]
- **Last line:** "By the name exchanged…descend, daughter of the gods—"[@vana-seidr.fm17-senses]
- **Name:** "Vana Seiðr."[@vana-seidr.fm17-senses]

A grey magic circle "that does not quite reach silver" unfolds; it shatters, and the fragments turn silver and are absorbed into her.[@vana-seidr.fm17-senses]

#### Effect {#vana-seidr-effect}

- **Freya's form:** she becomes the goddess in appearance and divinity, but cannot use Arcanum.[@vana-seidr.fm16-names, vana-seidr.fm17-prologue] As Freya she can also charm, though with less force and precision than the goddess herself.[@vana-seidr.fm17-charm]
- **Syr's form:** Syr is "one of Freya's many faces", and Hörn can take that form too.[@vana-seidr.fm18-form] Freya calls the spell's connection, made through her ichor, an "interesting side effect": it lets Hörn share in her divinity, "which also made it possible to reproduce the girl’s face".[@vana-seidr.fm17-prologue]
- **Shared senses:** while it is active Hörn shares all of Freya's senses and feels her emotions in a one-sided connection.[@vana-seidr.fm16-names] Freya can perceive through Hörn as well: in DanMachi 18 she sometimes sees the dreams of the sleeping Hörn.[@vana-seidr.fm18-senses]
- **Afterwards:** even when the spell is not active, a slight trace of Freya's emotions can flow into Hörn through her right eye.[@vana-seidr.fm17-eye]

#### Notable uses {#vana-seidr-notable-uses}

| Volume | Use |
|---|---|
| DanMachi 17 | Gravely wounded and summoning the last of her Mind, Hörn uses it to connect Bell with the goddess's feelings: she takes Syr's form and speaks the buried feelings of "the real her".[@vana-seidr.fm17-senses] |
| DanMachi 18 | After turning a blade on herself, she lies unconscious in Syr's form with the spell still active. Heith guesses that she keeps it going "to avoid losing something", but calls that only a guess.[@vana-seidr.fm18-form] Because the senses are linked, [[bete-loga|Bete]] keeps his voice down beside her so that Freya will not notice.[@vana-seidr.fm18-senses] |

#### Open questions {#vana-seidr-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - How long it can last, its Mind cost, and whether the three printed pieces are the whole chant.[@vana-seidr.fm17-senses, vana-seidr.fm18-form]

## Spells of rivals and enemies {#rivals-spells}

### Satanas Verion {#satanas-verion}

**Satanas Verion** is the attack magic of [[alfia|Alfia]], called "the Silence". Its ultrashort chant is a single word, *Gospel*, and it releases an invisible wall of destructive sound; the text calls her magic sound "and nothing more".[@satanas-verion.ar03-name, satanas-verion.ar02-sound] It is one of three spells she shows, with [[#silentium-eden|Silentium Eden]] and [[#genos-angelus|Genos Angelus]].[@satanas-verion.ar03-genos-third]

- **User:** [[alfia|Alfia]][@satanas-verion.ar03-name]
- **Element:** Sound[@satanas-verion.ar02-sound]
- **Chant:** "Gospel" (ultrashort)[@satanas-verion.ar03-name, satanas-verion.ar02-sound]
- **Spell key:** Rugio, written *exploding sound* (detonates leftover energy)[@satanas-verion.ar03-rugio, satanas-verion.ar03-ja]

#### Incantation {#satanas-verion-incantation}

{{nocite}} The chant is one word and the name is two, so both are given in full.

- **Chant:** "Gospel." The one word is said *Gospel*; the Japanese writes it with the kanji for *gospel* or for *noisy*.[@satanas-verion.ar02-sound, satanas-verion.ar01-church, satanas-verion.ar01-ja]
- **Chant and name together:** "Gospel: Satanas Verion." The narration explains: "First came her usual ultrashort chant, and the true name of destruction followed."[@satanas-verion.ar03-name]
- **Spell key:** "Rugio."[@satanas-verion.ar03-rugio]

{{nocite}} In most scenes only "Gospel" is printed. Astrea Record 3 shows that this is the chant, not the spell's name; this wiki treats "Gospel" alone as a chant-only print of the same spell.

#### Effect {#satanas-verion-effect}

- **A wall of sound.** In Astrea Record 1 a single "Gospel" scatters Ganesha Familia's fighters, as many as twenty of them, in a church and throws [[shakti-varma|Shakti]] and Adi against the wall, while the hooded Alfia escapes. Shakti takes it at first for some kind of vacuum wave.[@satanas-verion.ar01-church]
- **Damage without a direct hit.** In Astrea Record 2 it hurls Kaguya into the ruins and shatters her sword. Lyra dodges the blast but is still knocked down, bleeding from her eyes, ears and mouth, and realises: "It wasn't wind or light. It was sound!!" Alfia agrees: "my magic is sound".[@satanas-verion.ar02-sound]
- **Speed and reach.** Lyra judges it an ultra-short chant with huge range, which even outclasses [[riveria|Riveria]] in a straight contest of magic.[@satanas-verion.ar02-sound]

#### Rugio {#satanas-verion-rugio}

In Astrea Record 3, after blasting [[gareth|Gareth]] away with Gospel, Alfia says "Rugio" as Lyu and Aiz leap into the space he has left, and the spot explodes. Lyu recognises a spell key: Alfia is not casting a new spell but detonating the energy left behind by the first. Lyu has never seen a spell key used this way.[@satanas-verion.ar03-rugio]

#### Defences {#satanas-verion-defences}

- Asfi's ear-pieces, a modified Silence Lyre (see [[silence-lyra|Silence Lyra]]), damp incoming sound waves; Astrea Record 3 says they were custom-made to counter Alfia.[@satanas-verion.ar03-lyre] Even so, her attacks keep throwing the protected fighters across the ground.[@satanas-verion.ar03-still]
- Riveria escapes one blast in Astrea Record 2 by leaping out of range with Aiz, but her ears still bleed.[@satanas-verion.ar02-westmain]

#### Full power {#satanas-verion-full-power}

Alfia's defensive enchantment, Silentium Eden, greatly weakens her own magic while it is up. In Astrea Record 3 she drops it, then says "Gospel: Satanas Verion", and the blast leaves Astrea Familia strewn across "a fan-shaped zone of death".[@satanas-verion.ar03-name, satanas-verion.ar03-death]

#### Failure {#satanas-verion-failure}

Late in Astrea Record 3 a "Gospel" aimed at Lyu does not fire; Alfia begins coughing up blood.[@satanas-verion.ar03-fail] Her illness is a negative Skill, [[skills#gif-blessing|Gif Blessing]], which keeps lowering her stats, stamina and Mind while it is active.[@satanas-verion.ar03-gif] She casts Gospel again later, so the failure is not a permanent loss.[@satanas-verion.ar03-again]

#### Notable uses {#satanas-verion-notable-uses}

| Volume | Use |
|---|---|
| Astrea Record 1 | Scatters Ganesha Familia in a church; throws Gareth into a building, after which he and Riveria recognise her.[@satanas-verion.ar01-church, satanas-verion.ar01-gareth] |
| Astrea Record 2 | Against Kaguya and Lyra; later stops the reinforcements on West Main Street.[@satanas-verion.ar02-sound, satanas-verion.ar02-westmain] |
| Astrea Record 3 | Gospel and Rugio against Loki and Astrea Familia; full power with Silentium Eden lowered.[@satanas-verion.ar03-rugio, satanas-verion.ar03-name] |

#### Open questions {#satanas-verion-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - Its range and Mind cost, and how long its leftover energy can wait for Rugio.[@satanas-verion.ar02-sound, satanas-verion.ar03-rugio]

### Silentium Eden {#silentium-eden}

**Silentium Eden** is [[alfia|Alfia]]'s defensive magic: an invisible enchantment that surrounds her and automatically cancels magic. She raises it with a single word, *Ataraxia*.[@silentium-eden.ar03-named, silentium-eden.ar01-riveria] For a long time her opponents take it for a barrier spell; in Astrea Record 3 Lyra works out that it is an enchantment that is always on.[@silentium-eden.ar03-passive]

- **User:** [[alfia|Alfia]][@silentium-eden.ar03-named]
- **Type:** Passive enchantment that nullifies magic[@silentium-eden.ar03-passive, silentium-eden.ar03-named]
- **Activation:** "Ataraxia"[@silentium-eden.ar01-riveria, silentium-eden.ar03-again]
- **Cost:** Mind; weakens her own magic[@silentium-eden.ar03-mind, silentium-eden.ar03-named]

#### Activation {#silentium-eden-activation}

- **Word:** "Ataraxia."[@silentium-eden.ar01-riveria, silentium-eden.ar03-barrage, silentium-eden.ar03-again]
- **Name:** Alfia herself calls the enchantment "my Silentium Eden".[@silentium-eden.ar03-named]

{{nocite}} "Ataraxia" is the word of activation and "Silentium Eden" the enchantment's name; they are not two spells.

#### Effect {#silentium-eden-effect}

- **Nullifying magic.** In Astrea Record 1 Alfia answers [[riveria|Riveria]]'s [[#wynn-fimbulvetr|Wynn Fimbulvetr]] with "Ataraxia": a sound rings out as if receding, and the blizzard vanishes against an invisible barrier "as though it was never there"; Riveria recognises it as nullification.[@silentium-eden.ar01-riveria] In Astrea Record 3 a barrage of fire, lightning and arctic wind disperses "as though colliding with an invisible wall".[@silentium-eden.ar03-barrage]
- **Always on.** Lyra points out that Alfia's gestures and one-word "casts" are for show: the flames on Alize's sword vanished where Alfia touched it, with no spell cast at all. It is a passive enchantment, "like your fire enchantment, or the Sword Princess's wind", protecting her from any magic. That is why she seemed to cast two spells at once.[@silentium-eden.ar03-passive] Alfia replies that understanding it changes nothing.[@silentium-eden.ar03-passive]

#### Costs {#silentium-eden-costs}

- **Mind.** Lyra guesses that an enchantment that nullifies magic must be draining Alfia's Mind, and that she must keep it up for as long as they fight; Alfia admits that "that Level Two prum has outwitted me for a second time".[@silentium-eden.ar03-mind]
- **Her own magic.** Alfia reveals the larger drawback: while the enchantment protects her, all magic is nullified, including her own. It cannot cancel it completely, but it "drastically reduces the power of my own magic". Her silence, she says, is no armour but a seal to hold down hateful sound.[@silentium-eden.ar03-named] When she drops it, her attack spell hits at full strength (see [[#satanas-verion|Satanas Verion]]).[@silentium-eden.ar03-named]
- **Switching.** Lyra later says that if raising it takes an instant with an ultrashort chant, dropping it at will takes an instant too, so Alfia can switch between attack and defence in the blink of an eye.[@silentium-eden.ar03-again]

Alfia's illness, the negative Skill Gif Blessing, also drains her Mind; the novels do not separate how much of her weakening comes from each.[@silentium-eden.ar03-gif]

#### The captured shield {#silentium-eden-the-captured-shield}

Lyra carries a shield that Perseus ([[asfi|Asfi]]) and the smith "Cyclops" made from a prototype shield (the Japanese word means *original form*) that Hermes gave them, the one [[zeus-and-hera-familias|Zeus]] is said to have had: the Aegis. When Lyra hits Alfia with a shield bash, it touches the enchantment; later she uses it to meet Genos Angelus, and the shield neutralises the spell before shattering. Alfia recognises the effect: "That was my Silentium Eden!"[@silentium-eden.ar03-shield] The shield was made to snatch the effects of magic, and it broke; Lyra does not learn the spell.[@silentium-eden.ar03-shield]

#### A report of full immunity {#silentium-eden-a-report-of-full-immunity}

In Astrea Record 1 a messenger reports that the witch seems immune even to the strongest "magical and nonmagical" attacks.[@silentium-eden.ar01-report] That is a frightened report; what the novels later explain is protection against magic, and Alfia dodges and parries physical attacks.[@silentium-eden.ar03-passive, silentium-eden.ar03-genos]

#### Open questions {#silentium-eden-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - How much Mind it uses, and whether it stops curses or Skills as well as spells.[@silentium-eden.ar03-mind, silentium-eden.ar03-again]

### Genos Angelus {#genos-angelus}

**Genos Angelus** is [[alfia|Alfia]]'s final spell. After an ultra-long chant, a gray bell appears above her and bursts, releasing a destructive sound over a wide area.[@genos-angelus.ar03-chant, genos-angelus.ar03-bell] [[riveria|Riveria]] and [[gareth|Gareth]] recognise it as the spell she used to finish off the Leviathan at the end of one of the [[three-great-quests|Three Great Quests]].[@genos-angelus.ar03-leviathan]

- **User:** [[alfia|Alfia]][@genos-angelus.ar03-chant]
- **Chant:** Ultra-long; six printed pieces[@genos-angelus.ar03-chant]
- **Range:** "Over one hundred meders"[@genos-angelus.ar03-bell]
- **Earlier use:** The finishing blow on the Leviathan[@genos-angelus.ar03-leviathan]

#### Incantation {#genos-angelus-incantation}

**Complete printed chant; original ellipsis preserved (AR03).**[@genos-angelus.ar03-chant]

> O cursed root of my blessing, O maledict birth. O sin of mine, my twinn’d half’s demise… There is no ablution, purification, or solace. Heaven’s bell begets my sin. O bugle of the gods. O harp of spirits passed, O melody of light; all traces of my unpardonable act. O wretched life of mine; the god’s most blessed craft, hearken now my hate! Here is my confession! The price of my sin, I pay in full! Hear the howl of the holy belfry!

{{nocite}} **Notes and other printed variants**

{{nocite}} The chant is very long and is printed in six pieces between scenes of the fight. 

In the Japanese edition the first three pieces agree in sense with the print, and the last three differ. The fourth is {{ja|箱庭に愛されし我が運命よ──砕け散れ。私は貴様を憎んでいる|hakoniwa ni aisareshi waga inochi yo — kudakechire. watashi wa omae o nikunde iru}}, *O my life, beloved of the miniature garden: shatter. I hate you*; she says *inochi*, *life*, and *fate* is the written meaning. The fifth is {{ja|代償はここに。罪の証をもって万物を滅す|daishō wa koko ni. tsumi no akashi o motte subete o messu}}, *Here is the price: with the proof of my sin I destroy all things*. The sixth is {{ja|哭け、聖鐘楼|nake, seishōrō}}, *Wail, holy belfry*.[@genos-angelus.ar03-chant]

- **Opening (excerpt):** "O cursed root of my blessing, O maledict birth."[@genos-angelus.ar03-chant]
- **Middle:** the chant speaks of her sin, of the sound of heaven, of the bugle of the gods, the harp of spirits and the melody of light as the brand of her sin, of her hatred of her own life, of the price, and of destroying all things with the proof of her sin.[@genos-angelus.ar03-chant]
- **Last line (excerpt):** "Hear the howl of the holy belfry!"[@genos-angelus.ar03-chant]
- **Release:** "Genos Angelus."[@genos-angelus.ar03-chant]

The narration calls it "a third incantation", "an ultra-long chant", and says it is neither her fast sound wave ([[#satanas-verion|Satanas Verion]]) nor her magic-nullifying enchantment ([[#silentium-eden|Silentium Eden]]).[@genos-angelus.ar03-chant] Alfia recites it with Concurrent Casting, dodging every blow Astrea Familia aims at her and striking back, so they cannot stop her.[@genos-angelus.ar03-chant]

#### Effect {#genos-angelus-effect}

Alfia points upward; far overhead appears an ash-silver object, unlike a magic circle, shaped like a huge bell. The bell shines, cracks and bursts, releasing "a howl of destruction". It shatters the ground, breaks trees and puts out every fire on the floor. The aftershock alone blows off the adventurers' ear ornaments and makes their eardrums bleed before the main blast arrives.[@genos-angelus.ar03-bell] "The effect range was over one hundred meders."[@genos-angelus.ar03-bell]

#### Stopped by Lyra {#genos-angelus-stopped-by-lyra}

The narration says it could be neither dodged nor blocked, but Lyra throws herself behind her shield. The shield neutralises the spell and shatters, and Lyra is thrown across the battlefield. It had captured Alfia's Silentium Eden during an earlier shield bash.[@genos-angelus.ar03-shield] Right after she unleashes it, the recoil of the enormous spell drains her and makes her cough blood, and Lyu and Alize rush in.[@genos-angelus.ar03-after] Her illness, Gif Blessing, also keeps draining her stats, stamina and Mind.[@genos-angelus.ar03-gif]

#### Open questions {#genos-angelus-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - Its Mind cost, and the details of the Leviathan battle, which is only recalled.[@genos-angelus.ar03-leviathan]

### Rea Ambrosia {#rea-ambrosia}

**Rea Ambrosia** is a fire spell of [[zald|Zald]], the "Glutton". It is shown once, in his final duel with [[ottar|Ottar]] during the Great Conflict: his weapon is wrapped in flames, and with one swing they spread through the whole arena.[@rea-ambrosia.ar03-cast]

- **User:** [[zald|Zald]][@rea-ambrosia.ar03-cast]
- **Effect:** Flames that clad his weapon[@rea-ambrosia.ar03-cast]
- **Chant:** Printed with the name (quoted in part below)[@rea-ambrosia.ar03-cast]

#### Incantation {#rea-ambrosia-incantation}

**All printed words, including the spell name; completeness unstated (AR03).**[@rea-ambrosia.ar03-cast]

> O Father, forgive me, for I thieve from the plate of the gods! Let my flaming tongue devour! Let my burning fangs consume! Rea Ambrosia!!

{{nocite}} **Notes and other printed variants**

As Ottar finishes his own chant, Zald roars his, told from Ottar's point of view:[@rea-ambrosia.ar03-cast]

- **Opening (excerpt):** "O Father, forgive me, for I thieve from the plate of the gods!"[@rea-ambrosia.ar03-cast]
- **The rest:** two short commands, to his flaming tongue and his burning fangs, then the name.[@rea-ambrosia.ar03-cast]
- **Release:** "Rea Ambrosia!!"[@rea-ambrosia.ar03-cast]

{{nocite}} The novel does not say whether this is the whole chant or how long it is.

#### Effect {#rea-ambrosia-effect}

Zald's weapon is clad in "the fires of calamity"; one swing spreads the fire through the arena, and the wall of ice around them begins to melt and crack.[@rea-ambrosia.ar03-cast] His flaming blade then meets Ottar's weapon, golden with [[#hildis-vini|Hildis Vini]]. The collision sends shock waves through the city and brings down the ice wall and barrier, but that is the result of both attacks together.[@rea-ambrosia.ar03-clash] Zald loses the duel.[@rea-ambrosia.ar03-defeat]

#### Not Deus Ambrosia {#rea-ambrosia-not-deus-ambrosia}

Later in Astrea Record 3, drawing on the Guild's confidential records, Loki Familia explain that Zald has a Rare Skill called **[[skills#deus-ambrosia|Deus Ambrosia]]**, activated by eating: it is why he is called the Glutton. Gareth adds that Zald ate the flesh of the Behemoth to defeat it.[@rea-ambrosia.ar03-deus] That Skill is separate from the spell Rea Ambrosia.

#### Open questions {#rea-ambrosia-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - Its full chant, Mind cost and range, and whether Zald used it on other occasions.[@rea-ambrosia.ar03-cast]

### Dialv Dis {#dialv-dis}

**Dialv Dis** is a fire spell of Vena Dis, the younger of the Dis sisters: a silver-haired [[races#dark-elf|dark elf]] and vice-captain of [[apate-and-alecto-familias|Alecto Familia]], one of the most extreme groups among the [[evils|Evils]]. Her elder sister Dina, a white elf, is its captain.[@dialv-dis.ar01-sisters] Magic circles appear in the sky and release pillars of hellfire onto the targets below.[@dialv-dis.ar01-churches, dialv-dis.ar03-ten]

- **User:** Vena Dis, vice-captain of Alecto Familia ([[evils|Evils]])[@dialv-dis.ar01-sisters, dialv-dis.ar01-churches]
- **Effect:** Pillars of hellfire from magic circles overhead[@dialv-dis.ar01-churches, dialv-dis.ar03-ten]
- **Chant:** Ends "Open, the fifth garden! Resound, the ninth song!"[@dialv-dis.ar01-churches]

#### Incantation {#dialv-dis-incantation}

- **Printed ending:** "Open, the fifth garden! Resound, the ninth song!"[@dialv-dis.ar01-churches]
- **Release:** "Dialv Dis!"[@dialv-dis.ar01-churches, dialv-dis.ar03-ten]

In Astrea Record 1 these words complete a chant Vena had begun long before: the narration says it was "not an ultra-short cast, or any kind of special fast-chant ability". She had prepared the spell and held it ready ever since meeting [[hegni|Hegni]], fighting only with a magic sword in the meantime.[@dialv-dis.ar01-churches] The rest of the chant is not printed. In Astrea Record 3 only the name is printed.[@dialv-dis.ar03-ten]

#### Uses {#dialv-dis-uses}

- **The churches (Astrea Record 1).** As [[hedin|Hedin]] turns to rush back to the cathedral he has been guarding, four huge magic circles open over the cathedral and the other churches where survivors are sheltering. With Dina holding her hand and channelling energy into her, Vena calls the spell, and four columns of hellfire fall. Hedin's Valiant Hildr meets one and cancels it, saving the cathedral, but the other churches burn with the civilians inside.[@dialv-dis.ar01-churches]
- **The Amphitheatrum (Astrea Record 3).** With the sisters strengthened by Dina's curse [[#dialv-stige|Dialv Stige]], Vena calls Dialv Dis again: more than ten magic circles in all appear overhead and their pillars of hellfire trap Hedin and Hegni, whose evasion cannot shield them from blast waves from every side.[@dialv-dis.ar03-ten]

The number of pillars is not fixed: four in one scene, more than ten in the other.[@dialv-dis.ar01-churches, dialv-dis.ar03-ten]

#### The sisters' other magic {#dialv-dis-the-sisters-other-magic}

{{nocite}} These are separate spells, not versions of Dialv Dis:

- **[[#dialv-otua|Dialv Otua]].** Earlier in Astrea Record 3 the sisters together call out "Dialv Otua", after a chant about "the first gate", and balls of black fire rain like meteors on the city's defenders.[@dialv-dis.ar03-otua]
- **Vena's third spell.** At the Amphitheatrum, after the pillars of hellfire, Vena begins what the narration calls "the third and final spell of Vena Dis": an incineration spell, with a chant about "the sixth garden", that burns everything in her sight she considers heretical while sparing Dina. Hedin and Hegni pierce her magic circle before she finishes, and the uncontrolled spell explodes in an Ignis Fatuus.[@dialv-dis.ar03-third, dialv-dis.ar03-ignis]

#### Open questions {#dialv-dis-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - The full chant, how long it takes to cast, and whether Dina's help is needed.[@dialv-dis.ar01-churches]
> - How many circles the spell can make, and whether Vena's other unnamed flames at the Amphitheatrum are the same spell.[@dialv-dis.ar03-ten]

### Dialv Otua {#dialv-otua}

**Dialv Otua** ({{ja|ディアルヴ・オチュア}})[@dialv-otua.ar03-ja] is a spell of the Dis sisters, the twin leaders of Alecto Familia. In Astrea Record 3 they call out its name together from a rooftop in support of the [[evils|Evils]]' assault.[@dialv-otua.ar03-cast] Compare Vena's own [[#dialv-dis|Dialv Dis]].

- **Casters:** Vena and Dina Dis, together[@dialv-otua.ar03-cast]
- **Effect:** A rain of black fireballs[@dialv-otua.ar03-cast]
- **Chant:** Two short sentences[@dialv-otua.ar03-cast]

#### Incantation {#dialv-otua-incantation}

- **Chant and name:** "Let the first gate devour all. Turn all hope into despair! Dialv Otua!"[@dialv-otua.ar03-cast]

{{nocite}} The text does not say whether one sister chants and the other names the spell, or whether it belongs to one of them.

#### Effect {#dialv-otua-effect}

Balls of black fire fall from the sky like "meteors of Armageddon": walls fall and adventurers burn, while the smiths shoot down what they can with magic swords. In a moment the battle at the Amphitheatrum turns against Orario.[@dialv-otua.ar03-cast]

#### Open questions {#dialv-otua-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - Which sister the spell belongs to, and how it relates to Dialv Dis.[@dialv-otua.ar03-cast]

### Dialv Stige {#dialv-stige}

**Dialv Stige** is the curse of Dina Dis, a white elf, the elder of the Dis sisters and captain of Alecto Familia among the [[evils|Evils]].[@dialv-stige.ar01-sisters, dialv-stige.ar03-cast] It mixes Dina's basic abilities with those of the person she touches, and in Astrea Record 3 she uses it on her sister Vena to make them both more dangerous.[@dialv-stige.ar03-exchange]

- **User:** Dina Dis, captain of Alecto Familia ([[evils|Evils]])[@dialv-stige.ar01-sisters, dialv-stige.ar03-cast]
- **Effect:** Takes half the target's Strength and Agility, paying with her Defense and Magic[@dialv-stige.ar03-exchange]
- **Condition:** Touching the target[@dialv-stige.ar03-exchange]

#### Incantation {#dialv-stige-incantation}

**All printed chant words (AR03).**[@dialv-stige.ar03-cast]

> Black mire; red sin. We tear each other with our teeth; the slime that is our bodies mix’d!

{{nocite}} **Notes and other printed variants**

At the Amphitheatrum the sisters clasp hands and Dina chants. The narration stresses: "It was not a spell, but a curse."[@dialv-stige.ar03-cast]

- **Chant (excerpt):** "Black mire; red sin."[@dialv-stige.ar03-cast]
- **The rest:** one further sentence about tearing each other with their teeth and their bodies mixing.[@dialv-stige.ar03-cast]
- **Release:** "Dialv Stige!"[@dialv-stige.ar03-cast]

A crimson light covers Dina and spreads to Vena, and their stats shift "almost as though they were trading blood and flesh".[@dialv-stige.ar03-cast]

#### How it works {#dialv-stige-how-it-works}

The narration explains that the curse mixes her basic ability scores with those of whoever she touches. She can steal half the target's Strength and Agility; in return, since "no curse came without a downside", she must give the target an equivalent amount of her own Defense and Magic.[@dialv-stige.ar03-exchange] How the amounts are calculated is not stated. See [[status|Status]] for the basic abilities.

#### Used with Vena {#dialv-stige-used-with-vena}

For the sisters, the drawback works as an advantage: Dina fights up close with her twin stilettos, fast as an animal person, while Vena covers her with powerful magic, so the exchange strengthens each of their roles. The narration says the pair, both Level 5, approach the power of two Level 6s. [[hegni|Hegni]] thinks their curse is stronger than when they last fought; [[hedin|Hedin]] supposes they have been preying on their monstrous allies.[@dialv-stige.ar03-exchange]

When Hedin and Hegni swap opponents to attack the sisters' weaknesses, it fails: Dina knocks Hedin's weapon away before his short chant is finished, and Vena drives Hegni back with fire.[@dialv-stige.ar03-swap] Vena then calls [[#dialv-dis|Dialv Dis]].[@dialv-stige.ar03-swap]

#### Its weakness {#dialv-stige-its-weakness}

The cost shows at the end of the fight. After Hedin's thrust pierces Vena's magic circle and her breast, he fires Valiant Hildr as a single great beam through both Dina and Hegni. Hegni stays on his feet and cuts her down; Dina cannot withstand it, because "through the curse, she had traded away not only her Magic, but her Defense as well".[@dialv-stige.ar03-end]

#### Open questions {#dialv-stige-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - How long the exchange lasts, whether contact must be kept up, and whether it works on an unwilling target.[@dialv-stige.ar03-exchange]
> - Whether Dina's help to Vena in Astrea Record 1, holding her hand and channelling energy into her, is the same curse; it is not named there.[@dialv-stige.ar01-help]

### Shaldo {#shaldo}

**Shaldo** ({{ja|女王蜘蛛の巣}}, written *queen spider's web*)[@shaldo.so08-ja] is the only spell of [[valletta|Valletta Grede]], a commander of the [[evils|Evils]]. It covers an area with glowing reddish-purple circles; anyone who enters uninvited loses power and speed, and the more they move, the worse it gets.[@shaldo.so08-field, shaldo.so08-explain] In Sword Oratoria 8 she uses it as a trap for [[bete-loga|Bete Loga]].[@shaldo.so08-explain]

- **User:** Valletta Grede, of the [[evils|Evils]][@shaldo.so08-explain]
- **Type:** Status Down field ("barrier magic")[@shaldo.so08-explain]
- **Chant:** Super-long, by Valletta's account; not printed[@shaldo.so08-explain]
- **Ends if:** Valletta steps outside it[@shaldo.so08-explain]

#### Incantation {#shaldo-incantation}

The chant is not printed: when Bete walks in, the field is already in place.[@shaldo.so08-field] {{statement}} Valletta says it has a super-long chant.[@shaldo.so08-explain]

#### Effect {#shaldo-effect}

- **The field.** Faint reddish-purple geometric patterns cover almost the whole floor of the underground room, a circle 60 meders in radius centred on Valletta.[@shaldo.so08-field]
- **Status Down.** {{statement}} Valletta calls it a Status Down spell that saps the power and speed of any uninvited guest, and says the more they move around, the worse it gets.[@shaldo.so08-explain] Bete feels his reactions and strength fall by the second, and guesses his power has fallen below about Level 4.[@shaldo.so08-explain, shaldo.so08-level4]
- **No recasting.** The narration notes that it keeps lowering a target's Status by itself without being cast again, and notes that one, dozens or hundreds of people inside would all be affected.[@shaldo.so08-level4]

#### Costs and limits {#shaldo-costs-and-limits}

{{statement}} Valletta is frank about its drawbacks: the long chant, the Mind it uses, and the fact that it dissipates the moment she steps outside it. She calls it "not even that useful in real combat", but "perfect for a trap".[@shaldo.so08-explain]

She calls it barrier magic that blocks nothing; the narration calls it her barrier.[@shaldo.so08-explain, shaldo.so08-hati] It is a field that weakens those inside it, not a wall that stops attacks.

#### The trap {#shaldo-the-trap}

Valletta draws the enraged Bete into the middle of the field while her followers, all armed with magic swords, bombard him with fire, lightning and ice; one blast hits him as he tries to get out.[@shaldo.so08-level4, shaldo.so08-bombard] The elemental attacks come from the magic swords, not from Shaldo.

#### Broken by Hati {#shaldo-broken-by-hati}

Valletta expects Shaldo to drain even a transformed Bete if he keeps moving. Instead, her patterns begin to flicker as their power is sucked into Bete's [[#hati|Hati]], which can absorb attacks, curses and barriers, "anything that used magic power". Bete then strikes her down.[@shaldo.so08-hati] The narration had earlier supposed there was no way to break the field except her own conditions; Hati shows otherwise.[@shaldo.so08-level4, shaldo.so08-hati]

#### Earlier history {#shaldo-earlier-history}

In Astrea Record 1, set years earlier, the narration remarks that a mage, had one been there, would have sensed its traces.[@shaldo.ar01-valletta] That does not say when she gained Shaldo.

#### Open questions {#shaldo-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - Its chant, Mind cost and exact strength, and how "invited" people escape its effect.[@shaldo.so08-explain]

### Hal Reshef {#hal-reshef}

**Hal Reshef** is the curse of Seal, an elf who leads Resheph Familia, used against [[allen-fromel|Allen Fromel]] in Familia Chronicle 2. Through eye contact it makes its victim see their most beloved person and relive their painful memories; Seal uses it so that his followers can strike while the victim hesitates.[@hal-reshef.fc02-cast, hal-reshef.fc02-effect] Resheph is the name of Seal's god; the curse is printed *Hal Reshef*.[@hal-reshef.fc02-cast]

- **Caster:** Seal, leader of Resheph Familia[@hal-reshef.fc02-cast]
- **Type:** Illusionary curse, by eye contact[@hal-reshef.fc02-cast, hal-reshef.fc02-effect]
- **Chant:** "Run wild! Wind of pestilence!"[@hal-reshef.fc02-cast]
- **Ends:** After a set time; not when the caster dies[@hal-reshef.fc02-death]

#### Incantation {#hal-reshef-incantation}

{{nocite}} The chant is short and is given in full.

- **Chant:** "Run wild! Wind of pestilence!"[@hal-reshef.fc02-cast]
- **Release:** "Hal Reshef!"[@hal-reshef.fc02-cast]

Allen recognises from the chant that it is "not magic but a curse".[@hal-reshef.fc02-cast] When Allen later forces Seal to undo it, Seal cries "Be gooone, epidemic calamityyy!" as a removal; it fails (see below).[@hal-reshef.fc02-removal]

#### Effect {#hal-reshef-effect}

A dim, dark-purple light shimmers in Seal's eyes, and even Allen, fast enough to dodge whole barrages, cannot avoid "a ray of light that worked on eye contact".[@hal-reshef.fc02-cast] At first Allen finds no injury or ailment, and all the living soldiers vanish from his sight while the corpses and blood stay visible and his nose still smells the soldiers around him. Then a figure appears calling "Big Brother": his sister Anya ([[anya-fromel|Anya Fromel]]), printed here as *Ahnya*.[@hal-reshef.fc02-effect]

- **What the victim sees.** The narration names it an illusionary curse. Seal cannot know whom the victim sees, only that it is the person they love most; the curse revives the traumatic memories deep in the target's heart.[@hal-reshef.fc02-effect]
- **Mixed with reality.** The figures are real people wearing the illusion: the "Anya" walking towards Allen is an assassin with a poisoned dagger. The narration calls Allen's world "a mixture of illusion and reality", drawn from his own memories.[@hal-reshef.fc02-effect, hal-reshef.fc02-mixed]
- **Many figures.** When Seal sends his soldiers in, every one of them looks like Allen's sister: in her adventurer's gear, in her restaurant uniform, as a child.[@hal-reshef.fc02-attack]

Seal credits the curse for his Level 4: he has used it to defeat many warriors stronger than himself.[@hal-reshef.fc02-effect]

#### Against Allen {#hal-reshef-against-allen}

Allen strikes down the figure of his sister and then every sister-shaped soldier, and Seal cannot understand how Allen recognises him.[@hal-reshef.fc02-attack] {{statement}} Allen tells Seal he looks "just like some dumbass I hate more than anything in this world"; the curse was meant to show the person he loves most.[@hal-reshef.fc02-removal] Fighting through the illusion does not end it.

#### Removal and persistence {#hal-reshef-removal-and-persistence}

Allen threatens Seal into removing the curse. Seal speaks the removal and claims it has worked, but he has lost his reason and cannot control his magic well enough to undo it.[@hal-reshef.fc02-removal, hal-reshef.fc02-death] Allen kills him, and still sees his sister. The narration explains that the effect continues even after the caster is unconscious or dead, and disappears only after a set amount of time.[@hal-reshef.fc02-death]

#### Open questions {#hal-reshef-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - How long the curse lasts, its range and cost to the caster, and whether it affects monsters.[@hal-reshef.fc02-death, hal-reshef.fc02-effect]

### Kalima {#kalima}

**Kalima** is the curse of Argana, an Amazon of the goddess [[kali-familia|Kali]]'s Familia from [[telskyura|Telskyura]]. By drinking the blood of others blessed with a [[falna|Falna]], she strengthens her abilities; the goddess [[kali|Kali]] calls it *Blood Drain*. Argana also bears "Kalima" as her own alias.[@kalima.so06-explain, kalima.so06-history]

- **User:** Argana, of Kali Familia[@kalima.so06-explain]
- **Also called:** Blood Drain (by Kali)[@kalima.so06-explain]
- **Condition:** An ample amount of blood from someone blessed with a Falna[@kalima.so06-explain, kalima.so06-cost]
- **Cost:** Endurance drops significantly[@kalima.so06-cost]
- **Chant:** None printed[@kalima.so06-duel]

#### Name and classification {#kalima-name-and-classification}

Argana tells Tione that it is not magic like Bache's but "a curse", known as Kalima, and that Kali calls it Blood Drain; only Kali and Bache knew of it.[@kalima.so06-explain] Tione realises that Argana's alias, Kalima, came from it.[@kalima.so06-explain] The narration once calls it "a skill known only to Argana", in an ordinary sense of the word; it is not shown on a Status as a Skill, and this wiki follows Argana's own "curse".[@kalima.so06-cost]

#### Incantation {#kalima-incantation}

No chant or spell name is printed when it takes effect: in Sword Oratoria 6 Argana simply drinks Tione's blood, and in Sword Oratoria 12 she licks Tione's blood and the narration says she activates her Kalima.[@kalima.so06-duel, kalima.so12-knossos] Whether it needs any words is not stated.

#### How it works {#kalima-how-it-works}

{{statement}} Argana explains the rules to Tione:[@kalima.so06-explain, kalima.so06-cost]

- It strengthens her abilities with the blood of others who have a Falna.[@kalima.so06-explain]
- A splash of blood is not enough; it needs "an ample amount", like the mouthful she took from Tione.[@kalima.so06-cost]
- If the curse is broken, her abilities return to normal.[@kalima.so06-cost]
- The price is that her endurance "drops significantly".[@kalima.so06-cost]

The narration calls it a secret art that, its conditions met, boosts her Status without a ceiling at a steep cost in endurance.[@kalima.so06-cost]

{{nocite}} That is the narration's question; the novels give no figures and show no case of unlimited growth.

#### At Meren (Sword Oratoria 6) {#kalima-at-meren-sword-oratoria-6}

In her duel with Tione aboard a ship, Argana bites Tione's arms and neck and drinks her blood, and Tione's blows start to miss as Argana grows stronger.[@kalima.so06-duel] The cost shows later in the fight: Tione's Berserk makes her hit harder as Argana's defence falls, and Argana, refusing to cancel her curse, is beaten close to death.[@kalima.so06-cost-shown] [[finn-deimne|Finn]] then ends the fight with [[#hell-finegas|Hell Finegas]].[@kalima.so06-finn]

Tione recalls that in the Telskyuran rites Argana drank her opponents' blood, and even her own; the novel does not say whether her own blood powers the curse.[@kalima.so06-history]

#### In Knossos (Sword Oratoria 12) {#kalima-in-knossos-sword-oratoria-12}

Fighting on the same side, Argana asks Tione for blood; Tione tells her to take it, and Argana licks it, activates Kalima and gains strength. The two then pull on the ends of Tione's binding whip of light to hold down the spirit.[@kalima.so12-knossos] The binding is Tione's spell, not part of Kalima.

#### Open questions {#kalima-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - How much blood is needed and how long the effect lasts.[@kalima.so06-cost]
> - Whether her own blood can trigger it.[@kalima.so06-history]

### Velgas {#velgas}

**Velgas** is the magic of Bache, an Amazon of the goddess Kali's Familia and one of Tiona's teachers in Telskyura. It is an enchantment with the poison element: a blackish-purple light that coats her body and poisons anyone it touches. The narration calls it her only magic, and says it was dubbed the "Poison Queen".[@velgas.so06-cast]

- **User:** Bache, of Kali Familia[@velgas.so06-cast]
- **Type:** Enchantment; poison element[@velgas.so06-cast]
- **Chant:** "Die Asura" (ultra-short)[@velgas.so06-cast]
- **Coverage:** Right hand; whole body since Level 6[@velgas.so06-level6]

#### Incantation {#velgas-incantation}

{{nocite}} The chant is ultra-short and is given in full.

- **Chant:** "—Die Asura."[@velgas.so06-cast]
- **Release:** "Velgas."[@velgas.so06-cast]

Sword Oratoria 6 calls "Die Asura" an ultra-short chant and compares it with [[#airiel|Aiz's spell]].[@velgas.so06-cast] In Sword Oratoria 12 only "Velgas!" is printed.[@velgas.so12-spirit]

#### Effect {#velgas-effect}

A film of blackish-purple light forms around her right hand and thickens into a viscous, writhing spiral.[@velgas.so06-cast] It is poison, though it is described in terms of burning: blocking one of Bache's strikes with her arm makes Tiona's skin feel as if it is melting.[@velgas.so06-block] Where her hand touches the ground it leaves smoking, discoloured marks, and Tiona later coughs up blackish blood.[@velgas.so06-mark, velgas.so06-fever]

Bache kept it hidden during Tiona's training, using it only when she meant to kill, so that no opponent could prepare countermeasures or build up a resistance.[@velgas.so06-block] Kali sees that Tiona has picked up some status resistance in Orario (see [[development-ability#abnormal-resistance|Abnormal Resistance]]) but boasts that Dungeon monsters' venom does not compare with Bache's.[@velgas.so06-block]

#### Level 6 {#velgas-level-6}

Bache used to be able to enchant only her right hand. Kali explains that when Bache reached Level 6, her Velgas grew in power and in range, so that she can now cover her whole body, as Aiz does with Airiel.[@velgas.so06-level6] Bache herself says that her magic "may not be strong enough to act as armor", but it adds to her opponents' suffering.[@velgas.so06-level6] In practice it works defensively too: every contact, whether Tiona hits her or is blocked, poisons Tiona further, and Bache judges that without healing she has only about five minutes of fighting left.[@velgas.so06-defence]

#### Countered at Meren {#velgas-countered-at-meren}

In Sword Oratoria 6 the poison's damage keeps strengthening Tiona, whose Skills grow stronger as she is hurt. Her barrage wounds Bache so badly that Bache cannot keep Velgas up, and Tiona finishes her with a punch to the chest.[@velgas.so06-counter, velgas.so06-finish] Defeating Bache does not cure her: Tiona is still poisoned afterwards, and Lefiya says that healing her took considerable time.[@velgas.so06-after]

The narration says Velgas "guaranteed a swift kill".[@velgas.so06-cast] Tiona survives it, so that is a description of its reputation, not a rule.

#### In Knossos {#velgas-in-knossos}

In Sword Oratoria 12 Bache fights beside Loki Familia against a demi-spirit. She concentrates Velgas into one point on her fist; its poison rots a tentacle blackish purple, and the spirit detaches it. Other tentacles rot off from the poison as the fight goes on.[@velgas.so12-spirit]

#### Open questions {#velgas-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - Its Mind cost, how long it can be kept up, and how strong a resistance is needed to withstand it.[@velgas.so06-block, velgas.so06-defence]

### Phobetor Daedalus {#phobetor-daedalus}

**Phobetor Daedalus** ({{ja|迷暴呪詛}}, written *confusion-rampage curse*)[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-ja] is the curse of [[ikelos-familia#dix-perdix|Dix Perdix]], "a curse that mystified targets, confusing them".[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-explain] Anyone caught in it without protection is instantly thrown into a wild frenzy, attacking friend and foe alike until their strength gives out.[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-explain, phobetor-daedalus.so07-cast]

- **Caster:** Dix Perdix[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-cast]
- **Type:** Curse: confusion[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-explain, phobetor-daedalus.so07-cast]
- **Cost:** His own Status drops sharply while it lasts[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-cost]
- **Chant:** One short sentence[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-cast, phobetor-daedalus.so07-cast]

#### Incantation {#phobetor-daedalus-incantation}
