The chant is one short sentence, printed in two English wordings; both are kept.[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-cast, phobetor-daedalus.so07-cast]

| Print | Chant |
|---|---|
| DanMachi 10 | "Become lost in an endless nightmare." then "Phobetor Daedalus."[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-cast] |
| Sword Oratoria 7 | "Get lost in an endless nightmare!" then "Phobetor Daedalus!!"[@phobetor-daedalus.so07-cast] |

DanMachi 10 later prints the name alone.[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-explain]

#### Effect {#phobetor-daedalus-effect}

A wave of crimson light sweeps over the area. There is no explosion or shock, only "a hair-raising, malice-filled tone" in the ears, and then everyone it touched goes berserk.[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-cast] Its "extremely short trigger spell and wide range" make it deadly, and it has the most impact on first sight.[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-explain] [[bete-loga|Bete]] recognises it at once as a curse, "too undignified to be proper magic".[@phobetor-daedalus.so07-cast]

- **Secrecy:** anyone who witnessed the hunters became a monster's next meal, and a survivor would remember little from before the curse took hold.[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-explain]

#### Limits {#phobetor-daedalus-limits}

- **The price:** "My curse makes my Status drop like a rock," Dix admits; even with his Status a full Level lower, he still fights at Level 4.[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-cost] While it is active he cannot face [[lido|Lido]] on equal terms, but lifting it would free the maddened [[xenos|Xenos]] to turn on him.[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-lido]
- **One at a time:** he cannot cast it a second time without first lifting the curse already in effect.[@phobetor-daedalus.so07-bete]
- **Protection:** [[fels|Fels]]'s black robe, which protects against curses and anti-Status magic, shields Bell and two Xenos behind it.[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-explain]
- **Counter:** Bete's answer is to knock the victims out ("Sleep it off"), leaving no one for the curse to turn against him.[@phobetor-daedalus.so07-bete] Dix then realises that cursing Bete at point-blank range would make Dix himself the berserk wolf's first target.[@phobetor-daedalus.so07-bete]

#### Notable uses {#phobetor-daedalus-notable-uses}

| Volume | Use |
|---|---|
| DanMachi 10 | Used on the Xenos hunt: the monsters in range go berserk, and Fels's robe saves [[bell-cranell|Bell]]. It is the reason the hunters' crimes stayed hidden. Later the curse lifts and the Xenos come back to themselves.[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-cast, phobetor-daedalus.fm10-explain, phobetor-daedalus.fm10-release] |
| Sword Oratoria 7 | In Knossos Dix curses Bete's group; Bete escapes by diving into a side tunnel, and his companions and the monsters turn on one another.[@phobetor-daedalus.so07-cast] |

#### Open questions {#phobetor-daedalus-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - How long it lasts if Dix does not lift it, and its exact range.[@phobetor-daedalus.fm10-explain, phobetor-daedalus.so07-bete]

### Sharay's silencing curse {#sharay-silencing-curse}

**Sharay's silencing curse** is a curse cast by Sharay, a member of [[ishtar|Ishtar]] Familia, in Sword Oratoria 6. The novel gives it no proper name: "silencing curse" is the narration's description of what it does, which is to leave its target unable to use magic.[@sharay-silencing-curse.so06-curse] Sharay is the caster's name, not the curse's.

- **Caster:** Sharay, of [[ishtar|Ishtar]] Familia[@sharay-silencing-curse.so06-cast]
- **Name:** Not given; "silencing curse" describes it[@sharay-silencing-curse.so06-curse]
- **Effect:** Leaves its target unable to use magic[@sharay-silencing-curse.so06-curse]
- **Chant:** Not printed[@sharay-silencing-curse.so06-cast]

#### Incantation {#sharay-silencing-curse-incantation}

On Phryne's shout, Sharay, hidden on a nearby building, points her staff at [[aiz-wallenstein|Aiz]] and her companions, "her lips forming the words of a chant".[@sharay-silencing-curse.so06-cast] The words are not printed, so its incantation is unknown; it is not chantless.

#### Effect {#sharay-silencing-curse-effect}

A wave of high-pitched, high-frequency sound washes over them. Aiz escapes its range with no visible injury or status effect, but when she says "Awaken, Tempest" her [[#airiel|Airiel]] does not respond. She realises she has been cursed.[@sharay-silencing-curse.so06-cast, sharay-silencing-curse.so06-curse] The curse does not stop her speaking; it stops her magic.

Phryne takes the full blast too and is unchanged; Aiz reasons that Phryne has never been able to use magic.[@sharay-silencing-curse.so06-curse] Aiz had been saving Airiel for later, so the curse stops her casting it; the scene does not show it cancelling a spell already active.[@sharay-silencing-curse.so06-curse]

#### Curses in general {#sharay-silencing-curse-curses-in-general}

The same passage explains that a curse is a kind of jinx, different from "pure" magic spells. It weakens its target with witchcraft-like effects that ordinary magic cannot produce, at the price of a penalty on the caster, and only very limited methods can ward it off or lift it. The source adds that "Status effects were ineffective against it".[@sharay-silencing-curse.so06-curse] Sharay's own penalty is not described.

#### Ending it {#sharay-silencing-curse-ending-it}

Phryne says she prepared these curses and anti-Status measures for a fight with [[ottar|Ottar]] and finds Aiz a good test of them; she had meant to neutralise [[riveria|Riveria]] along with her, but Riveria sniffed her out.[@sharay-silencing-curse.so06-phryne] Aiz thinks the only way to break the curse is to defeat its caster, but Sharay has already fled.[@sharay-silencing-curse.so06-phryne] Later, after the fighting at [[ishtar-familia|Ishtar Familia]]'s retreat, the curse is undone and Aiz uses Airiel again; how it was lifted is not described.[@sharay-silencing-curse.so06-undone]

#### Open questions {#sharay-silencing-curse-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - The curse's name, chant, range, duration and cost to its caster, and how it was removed.[@sharay-silencing-curse.so06-cast, sharay-silencing-curse.so06-undone]

### Spells of the corrupted spirits {#corrupted-spirit-spells}

The corrupted spirits, or demi-spirits, that [[loki-familia|Loki Familia]] fights cast spells of their own. Sword Oratoria 4 calls Thunder Ray a bombardment spell allowed to the powerful spirits of the Ancient Times, and their chants are sung far faster than any human caster's.[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-firestorm, corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-thunder]

- **Casters:** Corrupted spirits (demi-spirits)[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-firestorm, corrupted-spirit-spells.so07-caelum]
- **Spells:** Seven named in the covered volumes[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-firestorm, corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-meteor, corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-thunder, corrupted-spirit-spells.so07-caelum, corrupted-spirit-spells.so12-faces]
- **Chants:** End with the spirit taking an elemental spirit's name as its own, then the spell's name[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-firestorm, corrupted-spirit-spells.so12-faces]

#### Incantation {#corrupted-spirit-spells-incantation}

**Fire Storm — printed ending only (SO04).**[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-firestorm]

> Your envoy beseeches you, Salamander! Incarnate of fire! Queen of flame—!

**Meteor Swarm — printed chant (SO04).**[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-meteor]

> Rise, rise, RISE! Husk of the earth! Sheen of iron! Hammer of the cosmos! May genesis’s pact upheave rock and stone! The sky shall burn! The earth shall split! The bridge shall rise! Heaven and earth shall become one! May the axes of the ether rain down and bring about calamity’s ruin—! Your envoy beseeches you, Gnome! Incarnate of the land! Queen of the earth—!

**Thunder Ray — printed words, initial ellipsis retained (SO07).**[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so07-thunder]

> …Pierce, spear of lightning! Your envoy beseeches thee, Tonitrus! Incarnate of thunder! Queen of lightning—!

**Caelum Veil — printed chant (SO07).**[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so07-caelum]

> Rage, fury of the skies.

**Icicle Edge — printed chant (SO12).**[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so12-faces]

> Freeze over as though the eternal permafrost, untold blades. Your envoy beseeches thee, Undine. Incarnate of water. Queen of aqua—

**Light Burst — printed chant (SO12).**[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so12-faces]

> Flash, rays of light. Tear through the darkness. Your envoy beseeches thee, Lux. Incarnate of light. Queen of luminosity—

**Dark Roar — printed chant (SO12).**[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so12-faces]

> Run wild, darkness. Devour the light in night’s peace. Your envoy beseeches thee, Shade. Incarnate of darkness. Queen of shadows—

Most of the printed chants end the same way: the spirit names itself the envoy of an elemental spirit, then the element's "incarnate" and "queen", and then the spell. Sword Oratoria 4 prints "Your envoy beseeches you, Salamander! Incarnate of fire! Queen of flame—!"; Sword Oratoria 7 and 12 print "beseeches thee".[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-firestorm, corrupted-spirit-spells.so07-thunder, corrupted-spirit-spells.so12-faces] The Japanese petitions no one: the spirit declares itself the deputy and takes the elemental spirit's name as its own, as an elf's chant ends "my name is Alf". Fire Storm ends {{ja|代行者ノ名ニオイテ命ジル与エラレシ我ガ名ハ火精霊炎ノ化身炎ノ女王|daikōsha no na ni oite meijiru, ataerareshi waga na wa Salamandā, honō no keshin, honō no ō}}, *I command in the deputy's name: the name given me is Salamander, incarnation of flame, sovereign of flame*: the spirit says *Salamander*, and *fire spirit* is the written meaning; it says *ō*, sovereign, over the written *queen*. Meteor Swarm ends the same way with *Gnome* (written *earth spirit*), and Thunder Ray, Light Burst, Icicle Edge and Dark Roar with *I who am the deputy, my name is* Tonitrus, Lux, Undine and Shade.[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-firestorm, corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-meteor, corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-thunder, corrupted-spirit-spells.so12-faces]

#### The spells {#corrupted-spirit-spells-the-spells}

| Spell | Element (spirit named) | What the text shows |
|---|---|---|
| Fire Storm | Fire (Salamander) | After "a protracted spell song", a tsunami of flame swallows the monsters, the floor and [[riveria|Riveria]]'s [[#via-shilheim|Via Shilheim]] dome, which cracks and shatters.[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-firestorm] |
| Meteor Swarm | Earth (Gnome) | After another long chant, the magic circle's light shoots up overhead and a swarm of black meteorites appears.[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-meteor] |
| Thunder Ray | Lightning (Tonitrus) | A short chant: beams of lightning burst from a magic circle. In Sword Oratoria 4 [[lefiya|Lefiya]]'s summoned [[#dio-grail|Dio Grail]] meets it.[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-thunder] In Sword Oratoria 7 a demi-spirit completes it with a short chant sung at a speed beyond human ken.[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so07-thunder] |
| Icicle Edge | Water (Undine) | A monstrous pillar or blade of ice that strikes instantly.[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-icicle, corrupted-spirit-spells.so12-faces] |
| Light Burst | Light (Lux) | A flash of light; in Sword Oratoria 12 [[mikoto|Mikoto]]'s gravity barrier intercepts it.[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so12-faces, corrupted-spirit-spells.so12-light] In Sword Oratoria 4 the spirit finishes it at the same moment Aiz releases her Li'l Rafaga.[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so04-light] |
| Dark Roar | Darkness (Shade) | "Oncoming darkness".[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so12-faces] |
| Caelum Veil | Lightning | One stanza, "Rage, fury of the skies.", makes a membrane of lightning around the demi-spirit like charged armour; the narration calls it an enchantment, like Aiz's [[#airiel|Airiel]]. With the word "Distel" ({{ja|放電}}, written *electric discharge*) the lightning shoots out in every direction.[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so07-caelum, corrupted-spirit-spells.so07-distel-ja] |

#### Casting {#corrupted-spirit-spells-casting}

In Sword Oratoria 12 the spirit pillar's main body chants a ritual to destroy the city while its three faces cast three different high-speed spells at once, Icicle Edge, Light Burst and Dark Roar, to fend off attackers.[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so12-faces]

#### Open questions {#corrupted-spirit-spells-open-questions}

> [!UNRESOLVED] Not settled by the covered English volumes
> - Whether every corrupted spirit can cast every spell, and whether "Distel" is a spell key like those of human casters.[@corrupted-spirit-spells.so07-caelum, corrupted-spirit-spells.so12-faces]
